function updateAdobeDataLayer() {
  const initialLength = Array.isArray(adobeDataLayer) ? adobeDataLayer.filter(item => item).length : 0;
  for (let i = initialLength; i < dataCollector.length; i++) {
    adobeDataLayer.push(dataCollector[i]);
  }
}
